  @ColorRect@3 <ColorRect>
    .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// Faint circuit-board traces with current pulses running along some of them.\n// rect_size must track the node's size (canvas units) so cells stay square.\n// drift slides the whole board (px per second) for a slow parallax behind\n// the panels; motion 0 (Reduce motion) freezes both the drift and the pulses.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(540.0, 960.0);\nuniform float cell = 46.0;\nuniform vec4 trace_color : source_color = vec4(0.22, 0.74, 0.97, 0.06);\nuniform vec4 pad_color : source_color = vec4(0.22, 0.74, 0.97, 0.10);\nuniform vec4 pulse_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 0.55);\nuniform float speed = 0.12;\nuniform float intensity : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 1.0;\nuniform vec2 drift = vec2(0.0);\nuniform float motion : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 1.0;\n\nfloat hash(vec2 p) {\n	return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);\n}\n\nfloat line_mask(float d, float half_w) {\n	return 1.0 - smoothstep(half_w, half_w + 1.0, d);\n}\n\n// Pulse head moving along 0..1 with a fading tail behind it.\nfloat pulse_at(float pos, float seed) {\n	float t = fract(TIME * motion * speed * (0.6 + seed) + seed * 7.0);\n	float behind = t - pos;\n	if (behind < 0.0) {\n		behind += 1.0;\n	}\n	return pow(clamp(1.0 - behind / 0.10, 0.0, 1.0), 3.0);\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec2 px = UV * rect_size + TIME * motion * drift;\n	vec2 id = floor(px / cell);\n	vec2 local = px - id * cell;\n	float h_row = hash(id);\n	float h_col = hash(id + vec2(17.3, 5.1));\n\n	float trace = 0.0;\n	float pulse = 0.0;\n	bool has_h = h_row > 0.42;\n	bool has_v = h_col > 0.62;\n\n	if (has_h) {\n		float m = line_mask(abs(local.y - cell * 0.5), 0.6);\n		trace = max(trace, m);\n		float seed = hash(vec2(id.y, 3.7));\n		if (seed > 0.55) {\n			pulse = max(pulse, m * pulse_at(fract(px.x / rect_size.x), seed));\n		}\n	}\n	if (has_v) {\n		float m = line_mask(abs(local.x - cell * 0.5), 0.6);\n		trace = max(trace, m);\n		float seed = hash(vec2(id.x, 9.2));\n		if (seed > 0.7) {\n			pulse = max(pulse, m * pulse_at(fract(px.y / rect_size.y), seed));\n		}\n	}\n\n	float pad = 0.0;\n	if (has_h && has_v) {\n		float r = length(local - vec2(cell * 0.5));\n		pad = 1.0 - smoothstep(2.6, 3.8, r);\n	}\n\n	float a_trace = trace * trace_color.a;\n	float a_pad = pad * pad_color.a;\n	float a_pulse = pulse * pulse_color.a;\n	float a = max(max(a_trace, a_pad), a_pulse) * intensity;\n	vec3 rgb = trace_color.rgb;\n	if (a_pulse > a_trace) {\n		rgb = pulse_color.rgb;\n	}\n	COLOR = vec4(rgb, a);\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(960, 960), shader_parameter/cell=null, shader_parameter/trace_color=null, shader_parameter/pad_color=null, shader_parameter/pulse_color=null, shader_parameter/speed=0.06, shader_parameter/intensity=0.4, shader_parameter/drift=Vector2(2.4, 1.2), shader_parameter/motion=1.0}
    .layout_mode = 0
    .anchor_right = 1.0
    .anchor_bottom = 1.0
    .mouse_filter = 2
    on resized -> lambda

        @PanelContainer@10 <PanelContainer>
          .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(8, 8), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.027450979, 0.039215688, 0.0627451), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
          .size_flags_horizontal = 3
          .size_flags_vertical = 4
          .mouse_filter = 2
          .theme_override_styles/panel = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=4.0, content_margin_top=4.0, content_margin_right=4.0, content_margin_bottom=4.0, bg_color=Color(0.03137255, 0.050980393, 0.09803922, 1), border_width_left=1, border_width_top=1, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.16470589, 0.23137255, 0.34117648, 1), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3), shadow_size=4, shadow_offset=Vector2(0, 2)}
          on child_order_changed -> lambda
          on visibility_changed -> lambda

            .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(44, 71), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
            .mouse_filter = 2
            .theme_override_styles/panel = StyleBoxFlat{bg_color=Color(0.043137256, 0.07058824, 0.12941177, 1), border_width_left=1, border_width_top=1, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.16470589, 0.23137255, 0.34117648, 1), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.42), shadow_size=10, shadow_offset=Vector2(0, 2)}

          @PanelContainer@61 <PanelContainer>
            .visible = false
            .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(2, 2), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.027450979, 0.039215688, 0.0627451), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
            .size_flags_vertical = 3
            .mouse_filter = 2
            .theme_override_styles/panel = StyleBoxFlat{bg_color=Color(0.03137255, 0.050980393, 0.09803922, 1), border_width_left=1, border_width_top=1, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.16470589, 0.23137255, 0.34117648, 1), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.42), shadow_size=10, shadow_offset=Vector2(0, 2)}
            on child_order_changed -> lambda
            on visibility_changed -> lambda
            on item_rect_changed -> lambda
            on resized -> lambda
            on size_flags_changed -> lambda
            on maximum_size_changed -> lambda
            on minimum_size_changed -> lambda

      @Button@81 <Button>
        .visible = false
        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(147, 52), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(-0.0039215684, 0.10588238, 0.1490196), shader_parameter/bevel=0.14, shader_parameter/tint_to=Color(0.11764706, 0.2509804, 0.6862745, 1), shader_parameter/tint_amount=1.0, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 52)
        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
        .mouse_filter = 1
        .theme_override_styles/normal = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=16.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=16.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, bg_color=Color(0.011764706, 0.4117647, 0.6313726, 1), border_width_left=1, border_width_top=1, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.05490196, 0.64705884, 0.9137255, 1), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0.22, 0.74, 0.97, 0.12), shadow_size=8}

    @ColorRect@96 <ColorRect>
      .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// Faint circuit-board traces with current pulses running along some of them.\n// rect_size must track the node's size (canvas units) so cells stay square.\n// drift slides the whole board (px per second) for a slow parallax behind\n// the panels; motion 0 (Reduce motion) freezes both the drift and the pulses.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(540.0, 960.0);\nuniform float cell = 46.0;\nuniform vec4 trace_color : source_color = vec4(0.22, 0.74, 0.97, 0.06);\nuniform vec4 pad_color : source_color = vec4(0.22, 0.74, 0.97, 0.10);\nuniform vec4 pulse_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 0.55);\nuniform float speed = 0.12;\nuniform float intensity : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 1.0;\nuniform vec2 drift = vec2(0.0);\nuniform float motion : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 1.0;\n\nfloat hash(vec2 p) {\n	return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);\n}\n\nfloat line_mask(float d, float half_w) {\n	return 1.0 - smoothstep(half_w, half_w + 1.0, d);\n}\n\n// Pulse head moving along 0..1 with a fading tail behind it.\nfloat pulse_at(float pos, float seed) {\n	float t = fract(TIME * motion * speed * (0.6 + seed) + seed * 7.0);\n	float behind = t - pos;\n	if (behind < 0.0) {\n		behind += 1.0;\n	}\n	return pow(clamp(1.0 - behind / 0.10, 0.0, 1.0), 3.0);\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec2 px = UV * rect_size + TIME * motion * drift;\n	vec2 id = floor(px / cell);\n	vec2 local = px - id * cell;\n	float h_row = hash(id);\n	float h_col = hash(id + vec2(17.3, 5.1));\n\n	float trace = 0.0;\n	float pulse = 0.0;\n	bool has_h = h_row > 0.42;\n	bool has_v = h_col > 0.62;\n\n	if (has_h) {\n		float m = line_mask(abs(local.y - cell * 0.5), 0.6);\n		trace = max(trace, m);\n		float seed = hash(vec2(id.y, 3.7));\n		if (seed > 0.55) {\n			pulse = max(pulse, m * pulse_at(fract(px.x / rect_size.x), seed));\n		}\n	}\n	if (has_v) {\n		float m = line_mask(abs(local.x - cell * 0.5), 0.6);\n		trace = max(trace, m);\n		float seed = hash(vec2(id.x, 9.2));\n		if (seed > 0.7) {\n			pulse = max(pulse, m * pulse_at(fract(px.y / rect_size.y), seed));\n		}\n	}\n\n	float pad = 0.0;\n	if (has_h && has_v) {\n		float r = length(local - vec2(cell * 0.5));\n		pad = 1.0 - smoothstep(2.6, 3.8, r);\n	}\n\n	float a_trace = trace * trace_color.a;\n	float a_pad = pad * pad_color.a;\n	float a_pulse = pulse * pulse_color.a;\n	float a = max(max(a_trace, a_pad), a_pulse) * intensity;\n	vec3 rgb = trace_color.rgb;\n	if (a_pulse > a_trace) {\n		rgb = pulse_color.rgb;\n	}\n	COLOR = vec4(rgb, a);\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(960, 960), shader_parameter/cell=null, shader_parameter/trace_color=null, shader_parameter/pad_color=null, shader_parameter/pulse_color=null, shader_parameter/speed=0.06, shader_parameter/intensity=0.4, shader_parameter/drift=Vector2(-1.6, 2.2), shader_parameter/motion=1.0}
      .layout_mode = 0
      .anchor_right = 1.0
      .anchor_bottom = 1.0
      .mouse_filter = 2
      on resized -> lambda

          @PanelContainer@102 <PanelContainer>
            .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(383, 2858), shader_parameter/corner_radius=16.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
            .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(912, 0)

Works on the glyph alpha only.\nuniform float text_width = 400.0;\nuniform vec4 band_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float period = 3.6;\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nfloat hash(float n) {\n	return fract(sin(n) * 43758.5453);\n}\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	float t = mod(TIME, period) / period;\n	float x = local_pos.x / max(text_width, 1.0);\n	float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.09, abs(x - (t * 1.6 - 0.3)));\n	vec3 rgb = mix(base.rgb, band_color.rgb, band * 0.85);\n	float slot = floor(TIME * 12.0);\n	float flicker = hash(slot) > 0.985 ? 0.55 : 1.0;\n	COLOR = vec4(rgb, base.a * flicker);\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/text_width=null, shader_parameter/band_color=null, shader_parameter/period=null}

                    @Button@112 <Button>
                      .visible = false
                      .modulate = Color(1, 1, 1, 0)
                      .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(118, 64), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                      .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 64)
                      .mouse_filter = 1
                      .theme_override_styles/normal = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=20.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=66.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, bg_color=Color(0.043137256, 0.07058824, 0.12941177, 1), border_width_left=4, border_width_top=1, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.20392157, 0.827451, 0.6, 0.45), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3), shadow_size=4, shadow_offset=Vector2(0, 2)}
                      .theme_override_styles/pressed = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=20.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=66.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, bg_color=Color(0.03666667, 0.060000006, 0.11000001, 1), border_width_left=4, border_width_top=1, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.20392157, 0.827451, 0.6, 1), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3)}
                      .theme_override_styles/hover = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=20.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=66.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, bg_color=Color(0.090980396, 0.11705883, 0.17294118, 1), border_width_left=4, border_width_top=1, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.20392157, 0.827451, 0.6, 1), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0.20392157, 0.827451, 0.6, 0.28), shadow_size=10}
                      .theme_override_styles/hover_pressed = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=20.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=66.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, bg_color=Color(0.03666667, 0.060000006, 0.11000001, 1), border_width_left=4, border_width_top=1, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.20392157, 0.827451, 0.6, 1), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3)}
                      .theme_override_styles/focus = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=20.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=66.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, draw_center=false, border_width_left=2, border_width_top=2, border_width_right=2, border_width_bottom=2, border_color=Color(0.49019608, 0.827451, 0.9882353, 1), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, expand_margin_left=3.0, expand_margin_top=3.0, expand_margin_right=3.0, expand_margin_bottom=3.0}

                    @Button@113 <Button>
                      .modulate = Color(1, 1, 1, 0)
                      .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(118, 64), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                      .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 64)
                      .mouse_filter = 1

                    @Button@115 <Button>
                      .modulate = Color(1, 1, 1, 0)
                      .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(118, 64), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                      .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 64)

                      @Button@139 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Open Book #1 • 25 questions  •  New"
                        .mouse_filter = 1

                      @Button@147 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Open Book #2 • 25 questions  •  New"
                        .mouse_filter = 1
                        .theme_override_styles/normal = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=12.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=12.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, bg_color=Color(0.043137256, 0.07058824, 0.12941177, 1), border_width_left=1, border_width_top=3, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.21960784, 0.7411765, 0.972549, 0.4), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3), shadow_size=4, shadow_offset=Vector2(0, 2)}

q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}

                      @Button@171 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Open Book #5 • 25 questions  •  New"
                        .mouse_filter = 1

                      @Button@187 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Open Book #7 • 25 questions  •  New"
